Result: VC-PQ与PQ^scene并报告的实验显示：VC-PQ能够区分并量化方法在视角一致性上的不足；分解分析揭示各方法分数损失来源（掩码、跨视角一致性或阈值相关）。受控扰动表明VC-PQ对“漏视图”和ID切换的惩罚随涉及视图数线性（或按视图计数）增加，而PQ^scene主要随像素面积变化。

Conclusion: VC-PQ使多视角全景分割评测显式纳入视角一致性，应与PQ^scene并行报告；其分解为诊断模型弱点提供工具，并通过可调参数与传统面积加权保持可比性。

Abstract: Multi-view panoptic segmentation assigns a semantic class and a scene-level instance ID to every pixel of an unordered set of images, and recent feed-forward 3D models predict these labels for the input views in a single forward pass. Their predictions, however, have been evaluated with the scene-level PQ (PQ^scene) borrowed from per-scene optimization methods, typically on rendered held-out views. PQ^scene tiles all views of a scene into a single image, so that a missed appearance or a change of ID lowers the score of the matched pair only in proportion to its area. We propose View-Consistent Panoptic Quality (VC-PQ), which extends PQ from a single image to a set of input views, counts equally every view in which an instance is visible, and penalizes a prediction that is not visible in the same views as its ground truth. A decomposition of VC-PQ attributes the score a method loses to mask accuracy, view consistency, and the matching threshold. A single additional parameter recovers the area weighting of tiling for comparison. Under a fixed evaluation protocol on ScanNet++ and ScanNetv2, recent feed-forward methods are evaluated with VC-PQ and PQ^scene, and the decomposition shows where each of them loses its score. Controlled perturbations of the ground truth show that VC-PQ responds to the number of views in which an instance is missed or changes ID, whereas PQ^scene responds to their area. The aim of this work is to make view consistency part of the evaluation of multi-view panoptic segmentation, with VC-PQ reported alongside PQ^scene.

</details>


### [167] [Prompt and Refinement: Asymmetric Mutual Learning for Infrared Small Target Detection with Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05918)
*Yimin Fu,Songbo Wang,Lizhuo Liu,Baicheng Pan,Zhunga Liu,Michael K. Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PAR：一种针对红外小目标检测在噪声标注下的稳健互学框架，通过SAM与从零训练的检测器互教、互校正标签，并引入局部对比正则与证据不确定性，逐步净化监督，显著提升多数据集、多噪声场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISTD任务依赖大量像素级精标，现实中昂贵且易含噪；噪声标签会诱导模型学习伪相关模式，严重影响小目标检测的可靠性与泛化。因此需要一种能在不可靠标注下仍能稳健学习并逐步修正标签的训练范式。

Method: 提出Prompt and Refinement (PAR) 框架：以预训练的SAM与从零训练的ISTD检测器作为不对称同伴，通过互教学将彼此预测作为对方监督掩膜的纠正线索；结合局部对比正则以契合红外小目标的局部显著性；将检测器预测转化为mask prompt以适配并微调SAM；在优化中引入基于证据理论的不确定性估计，降低高不确定样本对学习的干扰，避免自我确认循环并实现逐步标注净化。

Result: 在三个人工与真实噪声场景的ISTD数据集上进行大量实验，PAR在各种噪声水平下稳定取得SOTA，较现有方法在检测精度与鲁棒性上有显著提升。

Conclusion: 异质先验的互学与基于不确定性的标签自校正能有效对抗ISTD中的噪声标注，PAR可在弱可靠监督下逐步逼近真实目标特性，成为实用且可扩展的红外小目标检测训练范式。

Abstract: Existing data-driven infrared small target detection (ISTD) methods typically require large-scale datasets with accurate pixel-level annotations for model training. However, such labor-intensive requirements are difficult to satisfy in real-world applications due to the heavy reliance on expert knowledge and the inherently weak distinctiveness of infrared small targets. Consequently, the presence of noisy labels during model training is inevitable, which can severely mislead the learning of target perception toward spurious patterns. To address this challenge, we propose Prompt and Refinement (PAR), a label-noise-robust asymmetric mutual learning paradigm for ISTD. Specifically, PAR comprises a pretrained Segment Anything Model (SAM) and an ISTD-specific detector trained from scratch, which learn collaboratively through a peer-teaching scheme. Coupled with local contrast regularity, the predictions of the two asymmetric peer models are mutually exploited as rectification cues for the supervisory masks of their counterparts. The interaction between complementary inductive biases effectively prevents the label correction process from degenerating into the self-confirmation loop of a single model, enabling progressive refinement of the annotations toward intrinsic target characteristics. In addition, the detector predictions are utilized as corrective mask prompts to facilitate task-specific adaptation of the vision foundation model. Moreover, an evidential uncertainty estimation strategy is introduced into the optimization process to further alleviate the adverse effects of noisy labels. Extensive experiments under diverse noisy label scenarios on three ISTD datasets demonstrate that PAR consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [168] [UltraDub: Towards Authentic Dubbing by Unifying Visually-Steered Flow Learning and Trajectory Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05932)
*Gaoxiang Cong,Liang Li,Jianwei Wen,Zhedong Zhang,Zheng-Jun Zha,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UltraDub框架，通过视觉引导的流式学习与轨迹引导实现高保真、口型同步的视觉语音克隆；包含运动引导双重检索(MDR)与节奏锚定轨迹引导(RTG)两大核心，并发布DiverseDub基准，实验在四个数据集达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频配音/视觉语音克隆在多模态顺序条件下易破坏已有的时间与说话人线索，且推理时指导权重失衡常导致语义/发音更准但口型不同步。需要一种统一方法同时稳固时间对齐、保留说话人一致性并提升语言可懂度。

Method: 1) UltraDub统一框架：以视觉作为“连续运动”与“结构节奏”两类信号。2) MDR模块：以共享唇部运动残差作为查询，动态重校两路检索(语言内容与说话人风格)，并用独立的时序门控调节其贡献。3) RTG机制：训练外的(零训练)推理指导，在仅用视觉预测的中点评估分层多模态校正，安全地增强语义条件同时保持时间对齐。4) DiverseDub基准：覆盖多场景的“野外”视频配音评测集合。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上取得SOTA，展现更高的可懂度、说话人一致性与口型/节奏对齐；RTG在不重新训练的前提下提升语义约束且不牺牲时序；MDR有效抑制多模态条件干扰。

Conclusion: 通过将视觉作为运动与节奏双重先验，UltraDub在视频配音中兼顾语义准确、说话人一致与口型时序对齐，并具备通用、训练外可增强的推理策略；提供的DiverseDub促进更全面评测。

Abstract: Visual voice cloning requires intelligible, speaker-consistent speech synchronized with visible articulation. However, sequential multimodal conditioning can disrupt previously established temporal and speaker cues, while imbalanced inference guidance can improve linguistic accuracy at the expense of lip synchronization. In this paper, we propose UltraDub, a Unifying Visually-Steered Flow learning and trajectory Guidance Dubbing framework that leverages vision in two ways: as continuous motion for multimodal context aggregation, and as structural rhythm for trajectory rectification. Specifically, we introduce the Motion-guided Dual-context Retrieving (MDR) module, which continually recalibrates linguistic and speaker-style retrieval through shared lip-motion query residuals, utilizing independent time-conditioned gates to regulate their contributions. Furthermore, we propose Rhythm-anchored Trajectory Guidance (RTG), a training-free mechanism that evaluates hierarchical multimodal corrections at a visual-only predictive midpoint, safely strengthening semantic conditioning while better preserving temporal alignment. Finally, we construct DiverseDub, a multi-scenario benchmark to evaluate video dubbing in the wild. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UltraDub achieves state-of-the-art performance across four datasets.

</details>


### [169] [ReMem: Streaming Video Understanding With Long Context Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05940)
*Li Yiheng,He Xu,Wang Shaobo,Shao Ling,Lu Shijian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReMem，一种无需训练即可将离线VLM适配为流式视频理解的技术，通过双内存机制在任意长度视频上保持长程上下文并实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM多面向离线视频，流式场景需低时延与持续理解；已有记忆/压缩方法在长输入下会逐步丢失远程语境。作者通过探测实验发现长程信息保留不足是瓶颈，亟需既高效又能保留关键信息的适配方案。

Method: 提出ReMem（训练无关适配）：1) Streaming Context Memory（SCM）以查询无关的注意力持续压缩历史上下文，构建紧凑的长期记忆；2) Retrieved Vision Memory（RVM）在推理时从记忆中检索与当前查询最相关且最显著的视觉片段，将其与当前输入拼接以增强VLM输入，实现对任意长流的在线处理。

Result: 在多项主流基准上（涵盖流式视频与一般长视频理解）取得SOTA；相较已有方法在延长输入长度时更好地保留长程信息，并在不额外训练的前提下降低延迟、提升准确度。

Conclusion: ReMem通过“持续压缩+相关检索”的双记忆机制，在无需再训练的情况下有效适配VLM到流式与长视频任务，缓解长程信息遗忘并实现端到端SOTA，为低时延长视频理解提供实用方案。

Abstract: Despite their impressive performance on a wide range of video understanding tasks, current Vision Language Models (VLMs) are predominantly designed for offline scenarios and struggle to handle online streaming videos that demand low latency response. Several studies have explored memory and token compression strategies in an attempt to adapt offline VLMs for streaming video understanding tasks. However, through our probing experiment, we identify that most existing works tend to progressively lose long context information as length of input stream increases. To address this, we propose ReMem, a novel training-free adaptation technique that enables VLMs to process streaming videos of arbitrary lengths while improving their long context information retention capability. ReMem exploits memory from two perspectives, implemented as two core components. The Streaming Context Memory (SCM) continuously compresses historical context with query-independent attention. The Retrieved Vision Memory (RVM) then retrieves the most salient, query-relevant context from memory to augment the VLM's input. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed ReMem achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across a variety of widely used benchmarks, spanning both streaming video and general long video understanding tasks.

</details>


### [170] [JLD: Perceptual Distance Through A Jacobian Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05967)
*Shreshth Saini,Balu Adsumilli,Alan C. Bovik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需人工标注、基于冻结视觉编码器雅可比矩阵构建的感知距离JLD，在多数据集上超越LPIPS/DISTS等，并具备分辨率稳健与视频可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有感知距离要么基于像素误差忽视人类感知，要么拟合人工评分而受数据与分辨率限制（如DISTS在分辨率翻倍时相关性显著下降）。需要一种既具人类感知一致性、又不依赖人工标注、对分辨率鲁棒且可解释的方法。

Method: 冻结预训练视觉编码器，利用其雅可比矩阵J在早期局部补丁特征空间中找出最影响编码器输出的方向，形成固定度量张量E[J^T J]（雅可比透镜）；在像素空间诱导出pullback度量，定义新的距离JLD。仅用100张无标注图像一次性估计透镜；并提出加速版本JLD-fast。方法可自然扩展到视频。

Result: 在四个标准感知数据库上达到SOTA，稳定优于LPIPS、DISTS、PieAPP、DreamSim；在TID2013上透镜项相关性随分辨率翻倍几乎不变（0.850→0.845），整体相关性高；JLD-fast较LPIPS-VGG快约4倍，平均相关性0.911；视频上在Waterloo IVC 4K达到0.786相关性，显著优于VMAF的0.611。

Conclusion: JLD通过从冻结编码器导出几何度量实现无监督、可解释、分辨率稳健的感知距离，在图像与视频任务中均表现优异，且具备高效变体JLD-fast。

Abstract: Image compression, restoration, and generation all require a way to measure how different two images look to a person. Pixel error ignores how people see, while the most accurate perceptual distances are typically fitted to human judgments, tying them to a fixed data and resolution. For example, when image resolution is doubled, the correlation of DISTS with human scores on TID2013 drops from 0.815 to 0.717. We introduce the Jacobian Lens Distance (JLD), which derives its perceptual geometry from a frozen vision encoder rather than from human labels. JLD combines the locality of early patch features with the perceptual sensitivity captured by later encoder representations. Specifically, we use the encoder Jacobian to identify directions in the early feature space that most strongly affect the encoder output, producing a fixed metric tensor, $E[J^\top J]$, which we call the Jacobian lens. The lens is fitted only once from 100 unlabeled images, taking about 35 seconds. Locally, this construction defines a pullback metric in pixel space, giving JLD a clear geometric interpretation that can be directly analyzed on real images. Across four standard perceptual databases, JLD achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently outperforms LPIPS, DISTS, PieAPP, and DreamSim. JLD is also robust to changes in image resolution, on TID2013, its lens-term correlation remains nearly unchanged when the resolution is doubled, decreasing only from 0.850 to 0.845. We further introduce JLD-fast, which is $4\times$ faster than LPIPS-VGG while achieving a mean correlation of 0.911. Finally, JLD naturally extends to video, reaching a correlation of 0.786 on Waterloo IVC 4K compared with 0.611 for VMAF.

</details>


### [171] [Label-Free Coreset Selection with Foundation Models for Efficient Annotation in Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05987)
*Tuo Yin,Jennifer Dhont*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GCcore：一种无标签、无超参数、确定性的病理图像核集选择法，通过基础模型嵌入并贪心最大化全局覆盖，跨10个任务/数据集优于14个基线，且提供覆盖下界保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理AI需要大量专家标注，但标注昂贵且人力短缺；现有核集选择方法依赖在自然图像上调参，迁移性差、临床使用繁琐，缺乏稳健性与可解释的性能保证。

Method: 用任意病理基础模型将全数据集图像嵌入为特征；以“全局覆盖”为目标，采用贪心策略选取样本，使选中子集在嵌入空间尽可能覆盖整体分布；算法完全超参数-free、确定性，并为任意核集规模给出全局覆盖下界保证。

Result: 在10个任务/数据集（含全切片分类、tile分类、组织分割）上对比14个基线（含SOTA），GCcore在6个任务拿第一、9个任务进前三；同时展示其他方法对超参数敏感，排名可变动达5位；代码开源。

Conclusion: GCcore在无需标签与调参的前提下，高效稳定地选择具代表性的标注子集，具备理论覆盖保证与强实证性能，适合病理学实际应用并可减少标注成本。

Abstract: Computational pathology has the potential to improve clinical outcomes through a demonstrated increase in diagnostic and prognostic accuracy. However, the development and validation of deep learning algorithms still require annotated data, a costly procedure involving expert pathologists who already face critical workforce shortages. Existing coreset selection methods to optimize annotation efforts currently all rely on hyperparameters tuned on natural-image benchmarks that do not transfer to histopathology and are cumbersome to use in clinical practice. In this study, we present GCcore, a novel label-free coreset selection method that embeds every image of a dataset with any pathology foundation model and greedily selects the samples that collectively maximize the global coverage of the embedding space. The proposed method provides a lower-bound guarantee on the global coverage of the returned coreset for any coreset size, while being completely hyperparameter-free and deterministic. We demonstrate GCcore's superior performance over 14 baselines including state-of-the-art methods across 10 tasks and datasets spanning whole slide image classification, tile classification, and tissue segmentation, where it ranks first on six and within the top three on nine, while also demonstrating how existing methods can shift by up to five rank positions depending on their hyperparameter settings. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/OncoAI-ULBHUB/GCcore.

</details>


### [172] [From Transformation to Target State: Rethinking Query Representation for Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05993)
*Yihe Zhao,Songhe Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ASAP-CIR：将零样本组合式图像检索从“动态变换查询”改为“重构目标静态状态后再检索”，用冻结的多模态大模型生成包含整体与原子语义的目标表示，并在检索中综合对齐整体状态、落地原子约束与校准证据聚合，在CIRCO等数据集上显著提升，表明“意图表示方式”本身是关键设计维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CIR输入由参考图像+修改文本组成，但文本描述的是从源状态到目标状态的“转变”，而候选库是“已完成的目标状态”。零样本方法直接在预训练视觉-语言空间用转变式语言查询，会出现表征不匹配：需要压制或改变源语义时尤其不稳。作者希望缓解这种不对齐问题。

Method: 将零样本CIR重述为“先重构目标状态，再检索”。具体提出ASAP-CIR：不训练，使用冻结的多模态大语言模型生成目标的静态表示。该表示=多条整体性描述（holistic）+一组可变长、带权重的原子语义（atomic semantics）。检索阶段融合三部分：整体状态对齐、原子约束落地（grounding）、以及经过校准的目标证据聚合。并通过受控的纯文本诊断比较“目标侧静态查询”与“动态组合查询”。

Result: 诊断实验显示：在需要抑制/转换源状态语义时，目标侧静态表述比动态组合式表述更可靠。实证在FashionIQ、CIRR、CIRCO上验证了方法的有效性与局限性，尤其在多目标的CIRCO上提升最明显。

Conclusion: 在CIR中，检索前如何表示组合意图（静态目标重构 vs 动态变换查询）会显著影响性能，这一设计选择独立于、且同等重要于检索骨干。ASAP-CIR提供了无需训练、可与现有检索器搭配的有效原则。

Abstract: Composed image retrieval (CIR) aims to retrieve a desired target image from a query consisting of a reference image and a modification text. This task exhibits an unusual representational asymmetry: the modification text specifies a transition from the reference state, whereas retrieval candidates depict completed target states. This creates a representation mismatch for zero-shot methods that query pretrained vision-language spaces directly with transformation-oriented language. We study this mismatch and reformulate zero-shot composed image retrieval as target-state reconstruction followed by retrieval. We instantiate this formulation with ASAP-CIR, a training-free framework that reconstructs a static target representation using a frozen multimodal large language model (MLLM). The representation combines multiple holistic descriptions with a variable set of importance-weighted atomic semantics, thereby preserving both overall target identity and fine-grained visual constraints. Retrieval then integrates holistic state alignment, atomic constraint grounding, and calibrated target-state evidence aggregation. A controlled text-only diagnostic shows that target-side static query formulations achieve more reliable retrieval than dynamic composed query formulations, particularly when source-state semantics must be suppressed or transformed. Experiments on FashionIQ, CIRR, and CIRCO further characterize the effectiveness and limitations of this representation principle, with the clearest gains on the multi-target CIRCO benchmark. These results show that how composed intent is represented before retrieval is a consequential design choice, distinct from the choice of retrieval backbone itself.

</details>


### [173] [Investigating Query-Insensitive Behavior in Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06018)
*Eryk Kołodziejczyk,Alberto Presta,Karol Szurkowski,Michal Byra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示：当前时空视频定位（STVG）模型在查询无关甚至缺失文本时仍会给出看似合理的时空预测，说明其对语言信号不敏感，暴露评测与训练假设的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STVG研究默认每个自然语言查询都与视频相关，但真实应用会出现无关或缺失文本。作者希望检验与揭示在非理想条件下主流模型的鲁棒性与失败模式，并推动更加可靠的评测与方法设计。

Method: 以HCSTVG-v2与VidSTG等先进模型为对象，系统地在两种非理想设置下做实验：（1）无关查询（negative queries）；（2）文本缺失（移除/置空查询）。对比其输出与常规设置的差异，并分析数据集中的规律性偏差（如时空先验、目标显著性、类别频次与镜头结构），以解释模型为何在缺乏语义对齐时仍能产生“可信”结果。

Result: 发现模型在无关或缺失文本时，依然能给出与真实标注看似一致的时空边界，显示出显著的“查询不敏感”现象；这种表现与数据集内在规律（如常见目标的时空分布、场景共现与镜头节奏）相关，模型倾向利用这些先验而非依赖文本。

Conclusion: STVG当前范式存在被数据集先验“投机取巧”的风险，应引入面向负样本/无关查询的评测协议与训练机制，并在模型架构上显式建模与检验查询相关性，以提升对语言-视觉对齐的可靠性。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal video grounding (STVG) aims to localize objects or events described by natural language queries in both space and time. Existing STVG models are typically trained and evaluated under the assumption that each query is relevant to the input video. In this work, we challenge this assumption by studying the behavior of state-of-the-art STVG models under irrelevant queries and missing textual input. Our experiments show that current models can still produce plausible spatio-temporal predictions even when the query is unrelated to the video or removed entirely. We further analyze HCSTVG-v2 and VidSTG to identify dataset regularities that may encourage such query-insensitive behavior. Our study highlights an underexplored limitation of STVG models and motivates negative-aware evaluation protocols and architectures that explicitly assess query relevance.

</details>


### [174] [Patch-based Querying Identifies Structures of Interest in Electron Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06020)
*Niels Vyncke,Nicolas Nadisic,Yvan Saeys,Aleksandra Pižurica*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自监督局部描述子的补丁级检索框架，交互式地在vEM体数据中用少量标注检索相似细胞结构，显著缩小后续分析的搜索空间，并在多器官胞器和多成像模态上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: vEM能在纳米分辨率下获取大体积3D生物样本，但产生的数据量巨大，后续预处理与标注高度依赖专家，成为分析瓶颈；需要一种高效、低标注成本的方法在数据中快速定位目标结构。

Method: 基于自监督学习获得稳健的局部图像描述子，对体数据进行补丁（patch）级表示与相似度检索；借助少量人工标注作为查询，通过交互式界面迭代精炼查询与结果，快速在全卷中定位自相似结构。

Result: 在真实vEM组织数据上，方法能稳定检出相关细胞结构，能跨不同细胞器类别与成像模态泛化；与人工全盘筛查相比，大幅缩小需要人工或下游算法处理的候选搜索空间。

Conclusion: 该交互式、自监督的补丁检索框架以少量标注实现对vEM数据中目标结构的高效定位，可靠且具跨模态、跨结构泛化能力，有助于显著降低下游分析的人力与时间成本。

Abstract: Volume electron microscopy (vEM) has emerged as an essential sensing technique in biomedical research, allowing the three-dimensional imaging of biological cells and tissues at nanometer-scale resolution. The ability to generate extensive datasets has reached the limitations of downstream analysis processes, which depend significantly on the intervention of human experts for preprocessing and annotation. We propose an efficient and reliable patch-based retrieval framework based on self-supervised learning of local image descriptors to locate self-similar structures in vEM datasets. Given a few manual annotations of a given cellular structure, our method can retrieve similar structures across the EM volume. Our framework is interactive, allowing the human expert to refine the search queries and retrieve relevant image patches quickly and using little labeled data. Experiments on real-world vEM images of biological tissues demonstrate that our framework can reliably identify relevant cellular structures, generalize across different organelles and acquisition modalities, and substantially reduce the search space for downstream analysis.

</details>


### [175] [Scalable Minimal-Change Learning for Controllable Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06021)
*Shuo Chen,Fengming Huang,Yu Yao,Mingming Gong,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出ARRO：用强化学习优化按指令图像编辑，使改动最小且符合指令，在多个基准显著提升编辑质量并减少无关改动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有按指令图像编辑常产生“无意更改”，而以潜空间L1等正则作为“最小改动”的代理在非线性生成器中失效且需难以规模化的监督。需要一种无需逐指令标注、能直接优化“只改该改的”的方法。

Method: 将最小改动与指令一致性作为RL优化目标。构建具代理性的视觉-语言奖励模型，分别检查三元组（源图、指令、编辑图）的两类失败：未实现的请求变更与无意更改。设计群组级rubric，对一组候选编辑进行合并与验证，生成稳定一致的奖励信号，无需逐指令人工标注；据此对编辑器进行RL优化，并配以SFT与对照实验。

Result: 在FLUX.1 Kontext-dev基础上，ARRO将跨MinEval、MagicBrush、AnyBench、Emu-Edit的平均EditScore从5.21提升到5.88；在600个评例上相对基线将非目标像素改动减少8.4%。还在奖励/SFT对照、盲评、人类评价与迁移到OmniGen2上得到佐证。

Conclusion: 通过奖励建模与群组rubric驱动的RL，ARRO实现更精确、最小侵扰的指令编辑，广泛基准与人评均显示改进，且具有可迁移性；其方法避免了潜空间正则不足与大规模人工标注需求。

Abstract: Image editing should change only the attributes specified by an instruction while preserving everything else, yet current methods often make unintended changes. We treat this minimal-change principle as an optimization objective for instruction-based editing. Latent L1 regularization is a poor proxy for output locality in modern nonlinear generators and often requires supervision unavailable at scale. We instead optimize edit outcomes with reinforcement learning. An agentic vision-language reward model audits each source image, instruction, and edited image for two failure types: unimplemented requested changes and unintended changes. A group-level rubric merges and verifies these issues to provide consistent rewards across candidate edits without per-instruction human annotations. On FLUX.1 Kontext-dev, ARRO raises average EditScore from 5.21 to 5.88 across MinEval, MagicBrush, AnyBench, and Emu-Edit. On 600 evaluation examples, it reduces off-target pixel change by 8.4% relative to the base editor. Reward and SFT controls, blinded human evaluations, and transfer to OmniGen2 provide complementary evidence. Code: https://github.com/Showwwwwwwww/ARRO

</details>


### [176] [Casual Flash Lighting for Gaussian Splat Inverse Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06035)
*Jiamin Xu,Dongheng Wei,Jiarong Zhao,Qi Wang,James Tompkin,Weiwei Xu,Gang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 结合静态室内照明与闪光灯多视角拍摄，在2D高斯样本（2DGS）框架下实现几何、材质与光照的联合恢复，显著提升材质分解与重光照质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖静态照明进行逆渲染存在严重歧义；主动光源方案成本高、对环境要求苛刻。作者希望利用随手可得的室内拍摄（同一场景、不同视角、闪光开/关）来补充约束，在不增加硬件的情况下提升材质与光照估计的准确性与稳健性。

Method: 以2DGS重建为骨架：1）提出“GS锚定的漫反射场”，用哈希编码MLP在栅格化2DGS深度处查询，仅依赖世界坐标，保证跨视角一致；2）将“闪光残差”（开闪与关闪图像差）用于约束反照率与BRDF，避免被多视图alpha混合吸收；3）对静态照明采用延迟着色渲染，使其同样监督材质分解；4）在多视角、闪开/关影像上联合优化几何与材质。

Result: 在5个合成与3个真实室内场景上，相比6个近期基线，在漫反射颜色、反照率与粗糙度估计上更优；重光照任务PSNR较次优基线提升4.17 dB。

Conclusion: 将闪光与静态照明信息互补融合，并以GS锚定的漫反射场确保跨视角一致性，可有效缓解逆渲染歧义，提升材质分解与重光照质量，且无需专用硬件。

Abstract: Recovering geometry, materials, and lighting from photographs is highly ambiguous when only static illumination is available. Active-lighting setups reduce the ambiguity but require dark rooms or specialized hardware. Instead, we synergize both static and flash lighting from casual indoor capture, with the flash on or off, each from independent viewpoints. The flash residual constrains albedo and the BRDF, while static lighting captures grazing-angle specular highlights that flash misses. With a 2DGS reconstruction framing, our key contribution is a GS-anchored diffuse field: a hash-encoded MLP is queried at the rasterized 2DGS depth. As it depends only on world position, it is view consistent in 3D and allows the flash residual to drive material decomposition instead of being absorbed by alpha-blending drift across views. At the same time, we render static lighting with deferred shading such that it can also supervise material decomposition. On five synthetic and three real indoor scenes, our method outperforms six recent baselines on diffuse color, albedo and roughness material parameters, and in relighting where PSNR improves by 4.17 dB over the next-best baseline.

</details>


### [177] [Representation Disentanglement for Fair Chest X-Ray Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06041)
*Yujie Sun,Ruizhe Li,Xiaowu Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种单编码器框架，通过双层去相关与原型引导的跨群体对比学习，弱化CXR表征中的人口统计学结构；并提出新指标DRAR量化这种弱化程度。在CheXpert跨年龄/性别/族裔八个交叉群体上，减少公平性差距（EO与AUC gap）且AUC基本保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在胸部X光诊断中表现强，但模型表征常嵌入年龄、性别、族裔等人口统计学偏差，导致交叉群体（intersectional）间性能不公。现有方法往往忽略类内差异或仅处理单一偏差，缺少衡量“人口结构被削弱”的指标。需要一种既能减少人口依赖、又能保留疾病判别信息并可量化效果的方法。

Method: - 单编码器框架：共享特征提取。
- 双层去相关：在特征层面与预测层面同时对与人口属性相关的成分去相关，降低表征对人口统计学的依赖。
- 原型引导的跨群体对比学习：为每类疾病在不同人口群体中学习原型，通过跨群体拉近同病种、拉远异病种，兼顾类内（跨群体）变异。
- 指标DRAR：Demographic Representation Alignment Reduction，用于度量疾病表征中人口结构被削弱的比例，相对ERM计算减少幅度。

Result: 在CheXpert测试集34,809张、8个交叉群体、4个分类任务上评估：
- 平均Equalized Odds gap从15.41%降至10.86%。
- AUC gap从5.95%降至5.01%。
- 相对ERM的DRAR=59.04%，表明人口结构显著减弱。
- 平均AUC仅轻微下降。

Conclusion: 通过表征解缠与跨群体对比、双层去相关，可在基本保持准确度的同时显著降低人口统计学偏差，提升交叉公平性；DRAR提供了量化这一过程的新视角与度量。

Abstract: Deep learning has advanced chest X-ray (CXR) diagnosis, yet demographic biases in learned representations may contribute to performance disparities across intersectional groups. We propose a single-encoder framework combining dual-level decorrelation with prototype-guided cross-group contrastive learning to reduce demographic dependence while accounting for within-class variation. We further propose Demographic Representation Alignment Reduction (DRAR), a new metric that quantifies the reduction in demographic structure within disease representations. The framework is evaluated on four classification tasks using 34,809 CheXpert test images across eight intersectional groups, defined by age, sex and ethnicity. Compared with empirical risk minimization (ERM), our method reduces the mean equalized-odds gap from 15.41\% to 10.86\% and the AUC gap from 5.95\% to 5.01\%. Our method achieves a DRAR of 59.04\% relative to ERM, with only a slight decrease in mean AUC. These results demonstrate that representation disentanglement can reduce demographic bias and improve intersectional fairness. Code is available at \url{https://github.com/06Yujie/Fair-Medical-Imaging}.

</details>


### [178] [Local2Mesh: Spatially Localized Contour-to-Mesh for Left Ventricular Reconstruction from Sparse 2D Cardiac MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06052)
*Haoyu Wu,Ling Lin,Pascal Lefèvre,Ruizhe Li,Xiaowu Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Local2Mesh：从稀疏2D心脏MR轮廓重建3D左心室网格，通过几何对齐与局部轮廓到顶点建模，显著提升重建质量、功能估计与跨数据集泛化，并有助于下游疾病分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏切片的CMR存在层间错位与切片间局部空间信息不足，导致基于整体特征聚合的方法易丢失局部轮廓与3D表面之间的对应关系，限制3D左心室重建与下游分析精度。

Method: 提出Local2Mesh：1) 几何感知对齐模块，校正层间错位；2) 平面感知Local Router，利用顶点到切片平面的距离，将局部轮廓特征路由到模板网格顶点；3) 结合局部与全局轮廓特征，通过图卷积/图形变形对模板网格进行形变，实现无3D网格标注的3D LV重建；并进行跨数据集零样本迁移与下游分类评估。

Result: 在M&Ms-2与ACDC上，几何重建与功能指标估计优于现有方法；从M&Ms-2零样本迁移到ACDC表现出强泛化；重建网格用于疾病分类优于直接用稀疏轮廓。

Conclusion: 将几何对齐与局部轮廓到顶点建模结合，可有效缓解稀疏切片带来的错位与空间信息不足问题，提升3D LV重建质量并增强下游心脏分析实用性；方法具备良好跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) left ventricular (LV) reconstruction from sparse cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging remains challenging due to inter-slice misalignment and insufficient local spatial information between slices. Global aggregation of contour features may obscure local contour-to-surface relationships. We propose Local2Mesh, a spatially localized contour-to-mesh framework that deforms a template mesh to reconstruct 3D LV geometry from sparse 2D contours without 3D mesh annotations. The framework introduces geometry-aware alignment to correct inter-slice misalignment and a plane-aware Local Router that routes contour features to template vertices using vertex-to-plane distances. Local and global contour features then jointly guide graph-based template deformation for 3D LV reconstruction. Experiments on two public datasets, M\&Ms-2 and ACDC, demonstrate superior geometric reconstruction and functional estimation over existing methods. Zero-shot transfer from M\&Ms-2 to ACDC demonstrates strong cross-dataset generalization. Reconstructed meshes also improve disease classification over sparse contours, supporting their utility for downstream cardiac analysis. These results demonstrate that combining geometry-aware alignment with local contour-to-vertex modeling improves LV reconstruction from sparse 2D contours and supports downstream cardiac analysis. The code is available at \url{https://github.com/hwu918945-alt/loca2mesh}.

</details>


### [179] [ROT: Rotating Hidden States towards Contextual Vectors for Hallucination Mitigation in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06056)
*Yijing Du,Xiangcheng Zhan,Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ROT：在LVLM中于中间层检测语义偏离，并以保持范数的旋转将隐状态拉回由文本与视觉上下文张成的局部语义平面，并在后续层进行表示平滑，从而训练-free 减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力权重操控只间接影响最终预测层的语义，且实证发现幻觉token在中间层对文本与视觉上下文的相似度都异常偏低，呈“语境偏离”。因此需要直接在隐状态层面进行几何校准。

Method: 提出训练-free、按层工作的ROT：1) 在自注意与残差相加后的隐状态上，动态检测与文本/视觉上下文的相似度偏离；2) 对偏离向量执行保持范数的旋转，将其投回由多模态上下文向量张成的局部子空间（平面）；3) 在后续层引入表示平滑机制，稳定被校准的表示轨迹。

Result: 在多种数据集、不同架构与规模的LVLM上，ROT均显著降低对象幻觉，显示出跨模型的一致有效性与效率。

Conclusion: 通过中间层隐状态的几何重定向与后续平滑，ROT无需训练即可系统性缓解LVLM的幻觉问题，为更扎实的多模态生成提供高效、可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) frequently suffer from object hallucination. Existing training-free interventions primarily manipulate attention weights, which indirectly affect the deep semantics reaching the final predictive layers. In this work, we shift our focus to the hidden state vectors extracted after self-attention and residual addition. Empirical analysis reveals that hallucinated tokens do not simply over-rely on linguistic priors; instead, they exhibit an anomalous contextual deviation, showing significantly lower similarities to both textual and visual contexts in intermediate layers. Motivated by this, we propose ROT, a layer-specific, training-free framework. ROT dynamically detects semantic deviation in the middle layers and applies a norm-preserving rotation to steer the hidden states back toward the local multimodal context plane spanned by the contexts. For subsequent layers, a representational smoothing mechanism is introduced to stabilize the calibrated trajectory. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ROT consistently reduces hallucinations across various model architectures and scales, offering an efficient, geometry-driven solution for grounded generation.

</details>


### [180] [Vision Transformer Ensembles for Panoramic Street Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06063)
*Yunus Serhat Bıçakçı*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 比较多种预训练语义分割模型，在相近算力预算下挑选最佳方案，并通过多尺度与水平翻转的集成，在PalmCity街景全景分割挑战中夺得榜首；以轻显存、快推理、可复现实验为特色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 街景全景分割对城市环境精细理解重要，但数据集小、训练成本不均，导致模型选择与公平比较困难。作者希望在受限预算下系统评测现成架构，形成可复用的挑战参赛工作流，并给出可复现实验与误差分析。

Method: - 候选：DeepLabV3+、SegFormer、UPerNet、Mask2Former、DINOv3+线性解码器、以及基于DINOv3的Encoder-only Mask Transformer等共九个预训练系统。
- 评测：在近似相等的计算预算下比较；再对两名优胜者用三个随机种子、延长训练预算独立训练。
- 集成：对三套Encoder-only Mask Transformer的类别概率做等权平均；推理时多尺度（三个尺度）+水平翻转测试时增强（TTA）。
- 资源与工程：单卡NVIDIA RTX 5090；报告所有模型与推理变体、类别级错误、数据来源条件与可复现实验检查。

Result: - 公共验证集（84张）：mIoU 60.95%，mF1 71.16%。
- 隐藏测试榜：mIoU 57.08%，mF1 67.96%。
- 效率：249张测试图整体推理（含初始化与溯源检查）251.49秒；峰值显存占用2.70 GiB。

Conclusion: 在受控预算与严格可复现框架下，Encoder-only Mask Transformer（基于DINOv3）结合多尺度与翻转TTA及多种子集成，达成最优权衡并取得挑战榜首。研究提供透明的比较与完整工作流，为小数据场景下的街景分割模型选择与工程落地给出实证指南。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation of street panoramas can support detailed descriptions of urban environments, yet small datasets and unequal training costs make model selection difficult. This paper presents the system used for a first place submission to the PalmCity challenge in the leaderboard snapshot dated 5 October 2026. Nine pretrained segmentation systems are compared using approximately equal computation budgets. The candidates include DeepLabV3+, SegFormer, UPerNet, Mask2Former, DINOv3 with a linear decoder, and an Encoder only Mask Transformer using DINOv3. The two leading candidates are trained independently with three random seeds and longer budgets. Equal averaging of class probabilities from the three Encoder only Mask Transformer models, evaluated at three image scales with horizontal reflection, produces 60.95% mean intersection over union and 71.16% mean F1 on the 84 image public validation split. The submitted predictions receive 57.08% mean intersection over union and 67.96% mean F1 on the hidden test leaderboard. Producing all 249 test masks takes 251.49 seconds including model initialization and provenance checks on one NVIDIA RTX 5090. Peak allocated GPU memory is 2.70 GiB. The study reports all eligible models, all inference variants, class level errors, source conditions, and reproducibility checks, providing a documented challenge workflow with existing architectures.

</details>


### [181] [Anatomy-preserving unpaired cone-beam CT refinement for image-guided radiotherapy using pseudo-label guided diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06094)
*Qi Lai,Yutong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RefineCBCT是一种无需配对数据的CBCT图像优化框架，结合伪标签引导与短步扩散，显著减少散射、硬化、噪声与截断等伪影，同时保持患者特异的解剖结构；在LUNG TCIA与PELVIC TCIA上优于代表性GAN与扩散方法，提升MAE/RMSE/PSNR/SSIM并改善软组织一致性与边界清晰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床CBCT因散射、束硬化、噪声、截断等导致图像质量与CT数值不准确，且由于运动与解剖变化等原因难以获得与CT严格配对的数据；需要一种在真实的非配对训练条件下，仍能有效去伪影并保持解剖一致性的CBCT优化方法，以支持更可靠的放疗影像引导。

Method: 提出RefineCBCT：在非配对CBCT/CT数据上训练，采用伪标签（由规划CT等生成的结构/外观先验）进行引导，并引入短步扩散（short-step diffusion）以稳定、渐进地去除伪影与噪声，同时约束模型保留患者个体解剖。与代表性GAN与扩散基线在公开LUNG TCIA与PELVIC TCIA数据集上比较评估。

Result: 在LUNG TCIA上取得最佳综合指标：MAE 19.411、RMSE 62.758、PSNR 30.845 dB、SSIM 0.931；在PELVIC TCIA上取得最佳MAE、PSNR、SSIM：14.905、36.671 dB、0.876。视觉上条纹/阴影伪影减少，解剖边界更清晰，软组织均匀性提升；剖线与ROI分析更接近规划CT。代码开源，数据可从TCIA获取。

Conclusion: RefineCBCT在无需配对训练的临床现实条件下，能高效、有效地提升CBCT图像质量与CT数准确度，保持患者特异解剖，有望增强CBCT在影像引导放疗流程中的可靠性与实用性。

Abstract: Cone-beam computed tomography (CBCT) is widely used in image-guided radiotherapy, but scatter, beam hardening, noise, truncation, and other artifacts limit image quality and CT number accuracy. Paired CBCT and CT data are difficult to obtain clinically because of motion, anatomical changes, and acquisition mismatch. We present RefineCBCT, an unpaired CBCT refinement framework that uses pseudo-label guidance and short-step diffusion to reduce artifacts while preserving patient-specific anatomy. RefineCBCT was trained and evaluated on unpaired CBCT and planning CT data from public LUNG TCIA and PELVIC TCIA datasets and compared with representative GAN and diffusion based methods. On LUNG TCIA, it achieved the best results across all metrics, with MAE 19.411, RMSE 62.758, PSNR 30.845 dB, and SSIM 0.931. On PELVIC TCIA, it achieved the best MAE, PSNR, and SSIM, with values of 14.905, 36.671 dB, and 0.876. The refined images showed fewer streaking and shading artifacts, clearer anatomical boundaries, and improved soft tissue uniformity, with line profile and ROI analyses showing closer agreement with planning CT. These results suggest that RefineCBCT provides efficient and effective CBCT refinement under clinically realistic unpaired training conditions and may support more reliable CBCT use in image-guided radiotherapy workflows. Code is publicly available on GitHub, and the evaluated datasets are available from The Cancer Imaging Archive.

</details>


### [182] [Benchmarking CLIP for Zero-Shot Face and Periocular Gender Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06102)
*Fernando Alonso-Fernandez,Kevin Hernandez-Diaz,Jose Maria Buades,Josef Bigun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文评估CLIP在Adience数据集上零样本性别估计：全脸准确率95.54%，眼周区域（periocular）零样本初始偏向男性，通过阈值校准降偏后达85.29%。在线性SVM微调特征下略有提升至86.17%，但仍显著落后于全脸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无需任务特定训练的多模态预训练模型（CLIP）能否胜任性别估计，尤其在仅有眼周信息的受限场景中，并分析零样本推断中的偏差来源与缓解方法。

Method: - 使用三个CLIP骨干网络，采用图像-文本相似度与“male/female”提示词做零样本分类。
- 数据集：Adience（11,299张正面图像）。评估两种视角：全脸与仅眼周（periocular）。
- 诊断零样本在periocular上的性别偏置，定位于决策阈值/边界错配。
- 进行阈值对齐（threshold alignment）以校准决策边界。
- 在CLIP特征上训练线性SVM作轻量监督基线并与文献结果比较。

Result: - 全脸零样本：95.54%准确率，无需特定训练。
- Periocular零样本：强烈男性偏置；阈值对齐后准确率达85.29%。
- 线性SVM在CLIP特征上仅带来微小增益，periocular最佳86.17%，约比先前Adience文献高2.8%。

Conclusion: CLIP在全脸性别估计的零样本表现极佳；在仅眼周条件下任务更难，初始偏置主要因决策边界错配，可通过阈值校准显著缓解，但与全脸仍存在显著性能差距。轻量监督（线性SVM）带来有限提升，显示特征已较强且瓶颈在视域受限与阈值设定。

Abstract: We investigate CLIP for zero-shot gender estimation from full-face and periocular images. Three CLIP backbones are evaluated on 11,299 frontal images from Adience using image-text similarity with male/female prompts, achieving 95.54% full-face accuracy without task-specific training. For periocular, zero-shot predictions are strongly biased towards males, primarily due to a misaligned decision boundary. Threshold alignment substantially reduces this bias, reaching 85.29% accuracy. Linear SVMs trained on CLIP features provide only marginal gains, with a best periocular accuracy of 86.17%, approximately 2.8% above previous Adience results in the literature. Nevertheless, the gap with full-face performance confirms the greater difficulty of periocular gender estimation

</details>


### [183] [AnchorGen: Anchored Optimization for Customizable Generative 3D Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06135)
*Hantao Zhang,Oliver Heinimann,Jieke Wu,Yingxuan You,Emilien Seiler,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AnchorGen：在联合形状-草图潜空间上训练的整流流模型，使3D优化在保持草图风格的同时最小化空气阻力 surrogate。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D形状优化依赖生成先验维持几何有效性，但这些先验与输入2D草图无关，纠偏时会向高密度分布收敛，牺牲用户指定的风格/比例。需要一种能在优化中保留草图约束的生成方法。

Method: 训练阶段：在成对数据上，将形状潜码与草图潜码拼接，进行无条件整流流（rectified flow）学习，得到联合分布流形。无显式条件信号。推理阶段：以目标草图为锚，固定或约束其潜码，通过梯度下降在形状潜空间优化可微的“拖曳（阻力）”代理损失；同时用token级余弦相似度惩罚，使草图潜码贴合目标草图。由此迭代停留在与目标风格一致的子流形上。

Result: 单一模型即可：1) 在保持草图风格的前提下进行形状优化并降低阻力代理；2) 进行显式维度（部件/参数）编辑；3) 在仅给定草图时进行形状生成，避免偏离草图风格的模式坍缩式纠偏。

Conclusion: 通过在联合草图-形状潜空间上学习并在推理时施加草图约束，AnchorGen实现设计保真且物理目标导向的3D形状优化，同时兼顾编辑与仅草图到形状的生成。

Abstract: Engineering design often starts from a 2D sketch that fixes style and proportions, yet the subsequent 3D shape optimization relies on learned generative priors to keep the geometry valid. However, these priors are agnostic to the sketch: while they admit a valid design by correcting a drifted proposal back to its training distribution, they often correct it towards the high-density region, ignoring the specified design. We introduce \emph{AnchorGen}, a rectified-flow framework trained unconditionally on the concatenated shape and sketch latents of paired data. The learned manifold represents the joint distribution of shape-sketch pairs, so constraining the sketch component restricts the iterate to the sub-manifold of shapes consistent with a target style. Since training employs no conditioning signal, the constraint is imposed at inference: gradient descent optimizes the shape latent to minimize a differentiable drag surrogate, while constraining the sketch latent to remain close to the target sketch via a token-wise cosine penalty. A single model thereby supports design-preserving optimization, dimensionally explicit design edits, and sketch-only synthesis.

</details>


### [184] [Efficient Test-time Adaptation through Candidate Verification and Divergence Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06147)
*Seungmin Oh,Seunghun Kang,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于VLM测试时自适应的新范式：将预测校正视为候选验证（TTC），通过“假设-重构-校正”在无需训练与迭代的前提下提升准确率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在零样本迁移中易受目标域分布偏移影响。多数TTA方法在“预测侧”做调整（logits、原型、缓存、先验或统计量），常带来计算与内存开销。作者希望在不牺牲效率的情况下增强鲁棒性与泛化。

Method: 测试时对每个样本取top-k候选标签，逐一当作假设：在与该标签关联的潜在子空间（由记忆库存储）中重构该样本特征；度量“散度位移”，即假设性插入该样本后，该候选子空间及其与其他候选的关系发生了多大变化。正确假设导致位移小，错误假设导致位移大。最终选择累计散度位移最小的候选作为修正结果。方法训练自由、无需迭代优化。

Result: 在5种TTA设定与15个基准上（零样本分类、域泛化、少样本、base-to-novel、跨数据集），相较SOTA VLM-TTA持续提升准确率，同时最高实现约2倍速度提升、超过3倍CPU内存节省、以及最多1.4倍GPU内存节省，相比最低内存的训练自由基线。

Conclusion: 将VLM测试时自适应重构为“候选验证”而非“预测调整”的TTC框架，凭借训练自由与高效的散度位移度量，在多设定与数据集上实现更优的准确率-效率权衡。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong zero-shot transferability but remain vulnerable to target-domain shifts at inference time. Test-time adaptation (TTA) offers a practical remedy, yet most existing VLM-TTA methods follow a prediction-side adaptation paradigm. They use test samples to adjust logits, prototypes, caches, priors, or feature statistics, often incurring additional computational overhead. In this paper, we take a different perspective and reframe VLM-TTA as candidate verification rather than prediction adjustment. We propose Test-Time Correction (TTC), a hypothesis-based correction framework guided by a simple principle: hypothesize, reconstruct, correct. Given a test feature and its top-k candidate labels, TTC treats each candidate label as a hypothesis, reconstructs the feature within the corresponding latent subspace stored in a memory bank, and measures the resulting divergence shift. This shift quantifies how much the candidate subspace and its relations to other candidates change after the hypothetical insertion of the test feature. A correct candidate hypothesis induces only a small shift, whereas an incorrect one perturbs the subspace more strongly. TTC therefore corrects the prediction by selecting the candidate with the minimum aggregated divergence shift. This training-free candidate-verification mechanism avoids iterative optimization and provides a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Across five TTA settings and 15 benchmark datasets, including zero-shot classification, domain generalization, few-shot classification, base-to-novel generalization, and cross-dataset evaluation, TTC consistently improves accuracy over state-of-the-art VLM-TTA methods while achieving up to 2x speedup, over 3x lower CPU memory usage, and up to 1.4x lower GPU memory usage than the lowest-memory training-free baseline.

</details>


### [185] [Bayesian Optimization in Sequence-to-Architecture Latent Space for Zero-Shot NAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06167)
*Ondrej Tybl,Lukas Neumann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种将贝叶斯优化引入零样本NAS的大规模架构搜索方法：用VAE学习前缀编码的潜空间，并把多种零样本代理指标标量化为单一BO目标；在1万次迭代/单GPU约8小时内找到在分类、检测、分割三任务上达SOTA且满足参数约束的网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本NAS虽便宜，但多用进化算法，缺乏显式目标建模，近似随机变异搜索；而贝叶斯优化能建模并聚合信息，但在高维、离散、图结构的现代NAS空间上扩展性差，只能用于小网络。因此需要一种既能利用BO优势又能适配大规模NAS搜索空间的方法。

Method: - 将架构以新颖的前缀编码表示，并训练VAE重构该表示，学习连续潜在空间以缓解高维离散图搜索难题。
- 在潜空间上进行贝叶斯优化，使用高斯过程/代理模型建模目标。
- 设计代理标量化：把多种零样本性能代理（如梯度流、参数敏感性等）组合为单一可优化的BO目标。
- 用1万次迭代在单GPU上搜索约8小时。

Result: 在给定参数量约束下，所找到的单一网络在图像分类、目标检测、语义分割三项任务上均达到SOTA性能，相较以往零样本NAS与BO基线显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过VAE学习的潜空间与多代理标量化，使BO能够高效用于大规模零样本NAS，显著优于基于进化的近随机搜索，并在多任务上验证了可迁移、高效和效果优越。

Abstract: Zero-shot Neural Architecture Search removes the prohibitive cost of traditional NAS, but its search process is typically based on the evolutionary algorithm (EA); lacking an explicit model of the objective, it often resorts to a near-random search through mutation. Bayesian Optimization offers a principled alternative by modeling the objective and aggregating information across iterations, but scales poorly to the high-dimensional, discrete, graph-structured spaces of modern NAS, restricting its use to only small networks. In this paper, we bring Bayesian Optimization to zero-shot NAS for large-scale architectures by learning a latent space via a Variational Autoencoder trained to reconstruct a novel prefix encoding of architectures and propose a proxy scalarization that combines several zero-shot proxies into a single Bayesian Optimization objective. After only 10,000 iterations of the proposed search algorithm (8 hours on a single GPU), our method found a network architecture which under the given model parameter count constraints achieves state-of-the-art results on three separate tasks -- image classification, object detection and semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [186] [EORestore-Agent: Fidelity-Guided Agentic Restoration of Remote Sensing Images with Composite Degradations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06196)
*Heli Qi,Zeqi Zhou,Jingjun Yi,Kunyi Liu,Ziyang Lihe,Junjue Wang,Osamu Yoshie,Naoto Yokoya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EORestore-Agent：用可验证的“逐步决策”替代不可测的端到端目标，针对遥感复合退化自适应选择与排序工具；在Landsat-8合成基准上较最强一体化基线提升2.3–3.2 dB PSNR，并能迁移到Sentinel-2真实大气退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像常含多种并存退化（雾、云、噪声、模糊、低照度、低分辨率）。现有“一体化”模型在复合退化下趋同到窄PSNR带且无法在推理时验证决策优劣；无参考条件下难以判断何时、用什么、以何顺序恢复。需要一种可追踪、可验证的自适应恢复流程。

Method: 将恢复问题转化为“可验证的逐步轨迹”。1) 细调的视觉-语言模型检测并报告残余退化类型，并据此从相应工具池生成候选。2) 训练相对质量评分器（用全参考监督，在合成退化链上学得），预测从当前图像到各候选的PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS变化。3) 接受准则：所有预测变化均非负且PSNR预测增益为正；否则保留当前图像。通过逐步选择自然诱导出工具与顺序；记录并可解释。

Result: 在包含六类退化的Landsat-8合成基准上，对2–6种复合退化，较最强重训一体化基线PSNR提升2.3–3.2 dB；零样本自然图像Agent在18种设置中有17种PSNR低于退化输入。将学习评分器替换为无参考质量差异会损失1.1–4.6 dB。剩余有害步骤幅度小且集中在接受阈值附近。

Conclusion: 参考自由但可验证的逐步决策可有效处理遥感复合退化，显著优于一体化模型与零样本Agent，并具备可解释性与稳健性；方法能在不重训的情况下向真实大气退化（Sentinel-2）迁移。

Abstract: Remote sensing images often carry composite degradations, in which haze, cloud, noise, blur, low light, and low resolution coexist. Restoring them requires deciding which tool to apply, in what order, and when to stop, yet no clean reference is available at inference time to verify these decisions. All-in-one models trained on single degradations converge to a narrow PSNR band as degradations accumulate. To formulate real-world remote sensing restoration as a traceable trajectory, we present EORestore-Agent, which replaces this unmeasurable objective with reference-free, verifiable per-step decisions. A fine-tuned vision-language model reports all residual degradation types, whose tool pools are scored together, so the restoration order emerges from step-wise selection. A relative quality scorer, trained with full-reference supervision on synthetic degradation chains, predicts the changes in PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS from the current image to each candidate. A step is accepted only when no predicted change is negative and the predicted PSNR gain is positive. Otherwise, the agent keeps the current image. On a synthetic Landsat-8 benchmark with six degradation types, EORestore-Agent improves PSNR by 2.3 to 3.2 dB over the strongest retrained all-in-one baseline on composites of two to six degradations, whereas zero-shot natural-image agents fall below the degraded input in PSNR in 17 of 18 settings. Replacing the learned scorer with no-reference quality differences costs 1.1 to 4.6 dB. The remaining harmful steps are small and cluster near the acceptance threshold. Sentinel-2 examples illustrate transfer to real atmospheric degradation without retraining.

</details>


### [187] [MoCAR: Motion-code Coordinate-aware AutoRegression for Continuous Trajectory Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06210)
*Yiming Xu,Hao Cheng,Monika Sester*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoCAR 将轨迹预测转化为“下一步连续码”预测：用坐标感知的连续潜在空间自回归生成未来运动，避免传统离散化与多阶段候选管线，同时在 Argoverse 上达顶级表现并具备零样本迁移优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型可直接自回归地复用离散token，但运动轨迹是连续、多模态且参考系随轨迹演化，缺乏可复用的干净token。因此需要一种既能编码局部几何又能携带参考坐标变换的信息，以便进行稳定的自回归生成。

Method: 提出 MoCAR：1) 从端点归一化的轨迹片段学习连续“motion-code”，每个code同时表示局部轨迹形状与该段引起的参考系变换；2) 训练时以历史codes作前缀（teacher forcing），预测未来codes；3) 生成时在时间、地图、交互体与多模态因素约束下自回归生成codes，预测的codes保存在潜在记忆中；4) 解码得到的端点用于更新本地场景坐标，无需重新分词、候选目标或提案-细化流程；5) 通过弱KL正则、潜在对齐与tokenizer-预测器联合优化，确保潜在自回归稳定。

Result: 在 Argoverse 基准上以单阶段架构获得顶级性能；从 AV2 到 AV1 的零样本评测迁移性强；在转弯密集场景有显著提升；消融显示连续motion-code空间、潜在对齐、弱KL与联合优化对稳定性至关重要。

Conclusion: 将轨迹预测表述为坐标感知的连续潜在代码的下一步预测，能实现稳定、高效、无需繁琐候选与再分词的自回归推理，并在多基准和场景中验证有效与可迁移。

Abstract: Autoregressive generation is natural for language, where predicted tokens can be directly reused as the next prediction state, but trajectory forecasting lacks such a clean token: motion is continuous, multimodal, and expressed in local coordinate frames that evolve with the predicted trajectory. We present MoCAR (Motion-code Coordinate-aware AutoRegression), a decoder-only framework that casts trajectory forecasting as next-code prediction in a coordinate-aware continuous latent space. MoCAR learns a continuous motion-code space from endpoint-normalized trajectory segments, where each code jointly captures local trajectory geometry and the reference-frame transition induced by that segment. Historical motion codes are used as a teacher-forced prefix, future codes are generated autoregressively under temporal, map, agent, and mode interactions, and predicted codes persist in latent memory while decoded endpoints update the local scene context. This enables rollout without trajectory-space re-tokenization, trajectory queries, goal candidates, or proposal-and-refinement pipelines. On Argoverse (AV) benchmarks, MoCAR achieves top-tier performance with a simple single-stage architecture, transfers strongly from AV2 to AV1 in zero-shot evaluation, and improves on turn-heavy scenarios. Ablations confirm that the learned continuous motion-code space, latent alignment, weak KL regularization, and joint tokenizer-predictor optimization are essential for stable latent autoregression.

</details>


### [188] [Frequency-Decoupled Diffusion Guidance for Non-Blind Image Deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06221)
*Sihan Wang,Jinshu Huang,Haibin Su,Yunhua Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种频率解耦的后验引导，用扩散模型做免训练图像复原，按低到高频逐步激活测量频带，并对未激活频率施加强度感知的谱正则，同时用局部轨迹正则稳定采样；在多种非盲去模糊、含噪条件下取得高PSNR/SSIM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散先验的频率感知引导会逐步融入观测，但未显式区分“哪些频率应被激活”与“退化导致的频率衰减”，导致对未激活频段里的衰减差异建模不足，影响重建质量与稳定性。

Method: 1) 频率解耦：用低→高频日程确定当前激活的测量频带；2) 衰减感知谱正则：依据退化核推导的衰减图，对未激活频率分量施加选择性先验；3) 局部轨迹正则：抑制状态到干净图像之间的局部不规则偏移，稳定扩散采样；4) 固定端点能量下的KL正则化路径解释；5) 实践中用Tweedie插件近似进行局部能量校正，构造随时间变化的引导项。

Result: 在自然图像基准上的非盲去模糊任务中，相比现有方法，在更高测量噪声下依然获得更高PSNR与SSIM，表现稳健。

Conclusion: 频率激活与衰减建模的解耦、加上局部轨迹正则与Tweedie能量校正，可在无需额外训练的扩散后验采样中，稳定且高保真地恢复图像，特别适用于含噪的非盲去模糊。

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models provide powerful image priors for training-free posterior sampling in image restoration. To guide this sampling process, frequency-aware methods progressively incorporate measurement information across frequency bands, facilitating coarse-to-fine reconstruction. However, existing methods typically do not explicitly separate frequency activation from degradation-induced attenuation, leaving attenuation differences among inactive frequencies insufficiently modeled. In this work, we propose frequency-decoupled posterior guidance to separate frequency activation from attenuation-aware spectral regularization. Specifically, a progressive low-to-high frequency schedule determines the active measurement band, while a kernel-derived attenuation map defines a selective spectral prior over inactive components. To stabilize the sampling process, we also introduce a local trajectory regularizer that suppresses spatially irregular state-to-clean deviations. For a fixed endpoint energy, we provide a KL-regularized path-space interpretation. In practice, we construct time-dependent guidance through local energy corrections using a Tweedie plug-in approximation. Experiments on natural-image benchmarks demonstrate strong PSNR and SSIM performance across challenging non-blind deblurring settings, even at higher measurement noise levels.

</details>


### [189] [Joint Class-Time Learning for Video Classification with Multi-Instance Partial-Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06234)
*Lingyu Shen,Wei Tang,Fakhri Karray,Min-Ling Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PIVOTMIPL，通过联合“类别-时间”分配把部分标注消歧与视频时序证据分配耦合，实现更强的MIPL视频分类；引入占用正则的球面匹配、候选受限推断与双边际KL投影，并以单一KL目标训练；在多个数据与特征上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIPL同时存在实例与标签层面的不精确监督，典型于视频分类：包级候选标签无法指明哪个时间片对应哪个类，易导致类别-时间错配与监督稀释。现有方法缺乏显式的类别到时序证据对齐机制，影响学习效率与准确度。

Method: 1) 联合类别-时间分配：通过占用正则的球面匹配，在上下文化的视频特征上学习非均匀的时间质量（temporal mass），并抑制过度集中；2) 训练时的候选受限推断：在候选标签集合内重新求解分配，避免无关类干扰；3) 双边际KL投影：构建结构化教师，将动量平滑的类边际信念与当前提案的时间占用同时保留；4) 单一计划层KL目标：用一个KL损失对齐全空间预测与教师；5) 理论分析：刻画候选重求解与简单掩蔽的差异，并证明在其构造下，联合目标可分解为类边际监督与类条件的时间监督。

Result: 基于Breakfast、DoTA、FineAction自动生成候选标签构建VCMIPL基准，并在四种特征表示上评测。大量实验显示，PIVOTMIPL在效果（准确性）与效率（训练/推断开销）均超越现有MIPL算法。

Conclusion: 通过把标签消歧与时序证据分配统一到可学习的类别-时间匹配框架，并配合候选受限推断与双边际KL对齐，PIVOTMIPL在多基准与特征设置下实现显著优势，验证了结构化监督对MIPL视频分类的有效性与效率。

Abstract: Multi-instance partial-label learning (MIPL) addresses inexact supervision in both the instance and label spaces, which can be applied to video classification. However, bag-level labels do not explicitly supervise the correspondence between candidate classes and temporal evidence. We propose {\ours}, which couples label disambiguation with temporal evidence allocation through a joint class--time assignment. Occupancy-regularized spherical matching associates contextualized video features while learning nonuniform temporal mass and discouraging excessive concentration. During training, candidate-restricted inference recomputes the assignment within the candidate label set. A dual-marginal KL projection then constructs a structured teacher that incorporates momentum-refined class beliefs while preserving the proposal's temporal occupancy. A single plan-level KL objective aligns the full-space predictor with this teacher. Our analysis characterizes when candidate re-solving differs from masking and shows that, under the stated construction, the joint objective decomposes into class-marginal and class-conditional temporal supervision. We construct VCMIPL benchmarks from Breakfast, DoTA, and FineAction using model-generated candidate labels and evaluate the method across four feature representations. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that PIVOTMIPL outperforms existing MIPL algorithms in both effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [190] [CentriQ: Calibration-Free Quantization of Diffusion Transformers via Exact Mean Centering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06260)
*Nataša Jovanović,Mathieu Salzmann,Saqib Javed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出CentriQ，一种无需校准的数据无关量化方法，通过对每token居中+Hadamard旋转并用秩1全精度分支精确恢复均值，在DiT上实现4比特权重与激活的高质量生成，并在2比特设置下亦显著领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT采样开销大；4比特量化可降成本，但现有方法要么依赖特定校准数据与检查点（泛化弱），要么像Hadamard旋转的数据无关方法在DiT上显著掉质。根因在于自适应LayerNorm引入的每token均值在Hadamard旋转后仍沿主方向集中，主方向主导量化范围导致信息丢失。

Method: 提出CentriQ：1）对每个token先居中以去除均值；2）再做Hadamard旋转；3）通过一个秩1全精度分支精确恢复被去除的均值，使量化后激活仍保持原均值；4）据此无数据闭式求解每token量化尺度；5）权重量化用鲁棒ℓ_p（p<2）目标拟合，聚焦组内致密模态、抑制长尾；整体为校准无关、数据无关流程。

Result: 在三个DiT上，CentriQ在4比特下与需校准的SVDQuant质量持平，而传统“无校准权重量化+逐token激活量化”方案明显坍塌或退化；在2比特权重上超过此前最强的无校准方法，并首次在2比特激活下仍保持可用图像质量。

Conclusion: DiT的量化难点源于ALN导致的主方向均值，传统Hadamard旋转无法均匀扩散该均值。CentriQ通过居中+秩1恢复和鲁棒权重拟合，提供无需校准的数据无关4/2比特量化，在质量与泛化上优于现有无校准方案，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers (DiTs) achieve state-of-the-art image generation, but their sampling cost limits deployment. Quantizing both weights and activations to 4 bits reduces this cost, yet existing methods fall short in one of two ways. Calibration-based methods are tied to a specific checkpoint and prompt distribution, whereas data-free Hadamard rotation, effective for LLMs, loses quality on DiTs. We show that this loss has a structural cause. Adaptive layer-norm conditioning adds a per-token mean to the activations, and at the widths of the evaluated DiTs, the Hadamard rotations used by data-free methods cannot spread this mean uniformly across coordinates. A single dominant direction therefore survives the rotation and sets the quantization range. We introduce CentriQ, a calibration-free quantizer that centers each token before rotation and restores the mean exactly through a rank-1 full-precision branch, so that per-token scales follow in closed form without data. Weights are fitted under a robust $\ell_p$ objective that tracks the dense mode of each group and discounts heavy tails. Across three DiTs, CentriQ matches the quality of calibrated SVDQuant at 4 bits, whereas calibration-free weight quantizers with plain per-token activation quantization collapse or degrade substantially. CentriQ outperforms the strongest calibration-free method reported to date at 2-bit weights. It is also the first calibration-free method to retain usable image quality at 2-bit activations.

</details>


### [191] [VepAgent: Bridging Causal-Transition via Tool-Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Video Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06293)
*Qiutong Chen,Yuchan Guo,Zhenlong Yuan,Haobo Yang,Fangfang Lin,Xinyi Long,Yin Wang,Zijian Song,Rui Lan,Shi Qiu,Boyuan Pan,Yang Luo,Yuyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VepAgent：面向视频事件预测的代理式MLLM框架，通过因果跃迁推理与工具增强RL实现更稳健的未来事件预测；构建futurebench-4K链式思维SFT数据、开发诊断工具库、设计复合奖励；在多基准上SOTA，优于更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在视频理解中多依赖回顾式总结与文本先验，难以弥补未观测因果跃迁，导致视频事件预测时对未来推断不可靠。需要一种能主动建模从已观察终态到未来事件的因果推进，并在推理时补全缺失证据的方法。

Method: 1) 构建futurebench-4K：包含链式思维注释，显式刻画未观测中间状态的推理步骤，用于SFT弥合因果逻辑缺口。2) 设计代理式框架VepAgent：集成工具增强推理，包含状态跟踪、帧检索、区域放大等诊断工具，推理时动态调用以恢复缺失的时空证据、消解视觉歧义。3) 提出复合奖励的RL优化：联合优化预测准确性、因果连贯性与可靠先验，使模型依赖视觉落地而非表面文本相似。

Result: 在FutureBench与NEPBench上取得SOTA，显著超过更大尺寸的MLLM；实证表明工具增强与因果导向的未来推理有效。

Conclusion: 通过因果跃迁建模、工具调用与复合奖励的代理式范式，可显著提升视频事件预测的可靠性与可解释性，为面向未来的视频理解提供有效路径。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable potential in video understanding, yet their reliance on retrospective summarization and text-centric priors often limits their ability to bridge unobserved causal transitions when applied to Video Event Prediction (VEP). To address this, we propose VepAgent, an agentic framework that integrates causal-transition reasoning with tool-augmented reinforcement learning (RL) for robust VEP. Unlike prior methods that passively project future trajectories from historical dependencies, our approach explicitly models the logical progression from terminal observed states to future events. Specifically, we first construct futurebench-4K, a high-quality chain-of-thought dataset for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) that effectively bridges the causal-logic gap by structuring the deduction of unobserved intermediate states. Subsequently, we develop a diagnostic tool library integrating state tracking, frame retrieval, and region magnification, enabling the agent to dynamically augment reasoning with external tools to recover missing spatio-temporal evidence and resolve visual ambiguities during inference. Moreover, we propose a composite reward mechanism that jointly optimizes prediction accuracy, causal coherence, and reliable prior, compelling the agent to rely on genuine visual grounding rather than superficial textual similarities. Extensive evaluations on FutureBench and NEPBench datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, significantly outperforming larger MLLMs and validating the empirical effectiveness of our agentic, future-oriented reasoning paradigm.

</details>


### [192] [Wiring Matters: Injection Topology and Initialization of Affordance Heads in Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06318)
*Zijian An,Linhan Wang,Jiayan Wang,Shijie Geng,Ran Yang,Yiming Feng,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究如何在VLA策略中安全高效地加入稠密可供性监督；关键做法是对骨干网络施加stop-gradient，并通过可学习“桥”在中间层向动作专家注入特征，最终在不增加几乎任何参数的前提下提升到SOTA表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密可供性（affordance）监督能提供丰富的空间-语义线索，但直接与指令跟随策略联合训练常破坏语言对齐与策略性能。作者想弄清：如何把可供性头接入现代VLA而不伤害、甚至提升指令执行？哪些设计细节（梯度流、注入拓扑、初始化）最关键？机制上到底起什么作用？

Method: 在LIBERO基准上进行受控接线研究：在VLA骨干上接一个可供性头，对骨干加stop-gradient以阻断可供性损失的反向传播；从可供性头的中间特征经“桥”（可学习层）回注至动作专家。做2×2消融：注入拓扑（拼接concat vs 残差residual）× 桥初始化（零初始化vs随机/主动初始化）。并做两项探针：1) 用真值可供性作为输入；2) 推理时将桥置零，观察其是否为“惰性正则化器”或“承重部件”。

Result: 若允许可供性梯度回流到骨干，策略性能从93.1%降到85.5%；施加stop-gradient后性能恢复且可比较。2×2消融显示初始化比拓扑更关键；最佳是“主动初始化的残差桥”，达到96.2%，参数增加<1%，匹配三专家的AffordanceVLA(95.8%)。探针显示：喂入真值可供性反而有害；推理时将桥清零表明：零初始化的桥在训练期仅起正则化作用（惰性），而主动初始化的桥在推理期也被依赖（承重）。

Conclusion: 可供性监督可安全增益VLA，但前提是用stop-gradient保护骨干，并通过恰当初始化的残差式桥进行特征回注。初始化是主导因素；最优接线以极小参数开销达到SOTA。机制上，桥既可能仅充当训练正则，也可能在正确初始化下成为推理期关键通路；简单接线优于复杂多专家设计。

Abstract: Dense affordance supervision is an appealing auxiliary signal for vision-language-action (VLA) policies, yet naively co-training an affordance head can severely damage instruction following. We present a controlled study of how to wire such a head into a modern VLA on the LIBERO benchmark. Our recipe reads the backbone through a stop-gradient and re-injects an intermediate head feature into the action expert via a learned bridge. The stop-gradient is a precondition: letting affordance gradients reach the backbone drops the policy below the headless base (85.5% vs. 93.1%). With the backbone protected, a same-budget 2*2 ablation over injection topology (concatenation vs. residual) and bridge initialization (zero vs. random) shows initialization is the dominant lever. The best wiring, an actively initialized residual bridge, reaches 96.2%, matching the far more elaborate three-expert AffordanceVLA (95.8%) with under 1% extra parameters. Two probes explain the mechanism: ground-truth affordances fed as an input hurt, and inference-time zeroing shows a lazy bridge acts only as a training-time regularizer while an active bridge becomes load-bearing.

</details>


### [193] [Readout Blindness: VLM Scores Miss the Spatial Direction Their Frozen Encoders Retain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06324)
*Guangyuan Li,Tianming Du,Yan Jiang,Bihan Wen,Jiancheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练的新读出方法ADR，从冻结的CLIP式特征中恢复有向空间关系（如“左/右”），显著优于常用打分与部分复杂MLLM读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP类视觉-语言模型在定向空间关系判断上接近随机。作者将其归因为“读出盲视”：常用打分规则对主客体对称，方向信息在读出时被相互抵消，即便编码器内部仍保留方向线索。

Method: 提出反对称位移读出（ADR）：在冻结特征上对齐文本词与图像patch，定位对象质心，利用匹配对象质心间的带符号位移作为关系分数；不引入额外训练或参数。为消除文本与世界先验带来的虚高，提出“先验去膨胀”，通过将样本与无关图像配对构造零假设，报告由图像-文本配对带来的真实增益。

Result: 在多种编码器族与方向平衡数据集上，部署式常用打分仍接近随机，即使经微调；ADR明显提升方向关系准确率。相较更复杂的读出（含MLLM似然/聊天推理），ADR在无需训练、计算成本更低的前提下优于或接近其性能。

Conclusion: 方向信息仍存在于冻结的CLIP式特征中，关键在于采用反对称、几何感知的读出。ADR与先验去膨胀为评测与利用此信息提供了简单高效的方案；代码与评测工具将开源。

Abstract: CLIP-like vision-language models remain a cornerstone of multimodal systems, yet their scores stay near chance on directed spatial relations, such as whether one object is left of another. We call this failure readout blindness and analyze, theoretically and empirically, why deployed scores miss the direction: when scoring rules treat the subject and object symmetrically, direction cancels regardless of encoder training. Guided by this analysis, we introduce Antisymmetric Displacement Readout (ADR), which aligns caption words with image patches in the frozen features and scores each relation by the signed displacement between matched object centroids. Notably, ADR succeeds without additional training or learned parameters, thereby demonstrating that directional information remains in the frozen encoder. However, text and world priors can inflate accuracy, so we further introduce prior deflation, which measures the benefit of the image-text pairing as the grounded gain over a null that pairs each item with an unrelated image. Extensive experiments across encoder families show that ADR substantially improves over deployed scores, which remain near chance on most direction-balanced sets even for fine-tuned encoders. Compared with more complex readouts, ADR outperforms the evaluated MLLM likelihood readouts and is competitive with their chat inference at a small fraction of the computation. These results support our claim that directional information can be recovered from frozen features by an appropriate readout. Our implementation and evaluation kit will be publicly available.

</details>


### [194] [SPIN: Image Immunization Against Diffusion Editing via Single-Step Projection in Stochastic Neighborhoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06334)
*Fengming Gu,Jie Zhang,Zhongqi Wang,Qiankun Li,Shiguang Shan,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPIN：在扩散模型图像编辑中，通过对早期去噪状态的局部随机轨迹邻域进行一次投影预测，并优化输入微扰，使多种可能编辑结果偏离，从而实现稳健的图像免疫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型驱动的指令编辑强大但易被滥用；现有图像免疫方法要么需要昂贵的全轨迹反传，要么依赖中间目标且易被后续去噪抵消；单一路径推理反馈有限，难以兼顾多种去噪延续与未知指令。

Method: 从早期去噪状态出发，在同一指令下采样若干随机邻近状态；对每个状态执行一次投影以预测其清洁潜变量（无需全展开）；以清洁编辑参考为基准，优化受限幅度的输入扰动，最大化这些预测与参考的平均偏离，等价于在局部随机轨迹邻域上做鲁棒化的对抗优化。

Result: 在两个图像编辑器与六个评测指标上，SPIN在已见和更具挑战的未见指令场景中均显著优于对比方法，保护效果更强且更稳健。

Conclusion: 通过一次投影与局部随机轨迹增广，SPIN在无需全轨迹反传的前提下，实现对多种潜在编辑结果的广覆盖抑制，提供高效且泛化性强的图像免疫方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have greatly advanced instruction-guided image editing, while also raising concerns about unauthorized image manipulation. Image immunization addresses this risk by adding imperceptible perturbations to an input image to disrupt subsequent edits. Since editing requests are unknown at image release, protection should remain effective beyond the instruction used to construct the perturbation. Existing immunization methods either require costly full-trajectory backpropagation or use intermediate objectives whose effects may be weakened by subsequent denoising. Meanwhile, a single inference path provides limited feedback about alternative denoising continuations. To address these challenges, we propose \textsc{SPIN}, a framework for image immunization via one-step projection over local stochastic trajectory neighborhoods. Starting from an early denoising state, \textsc{SPIN} generates stochastic neighboring states under the same instruction and predicts their clean latents through one-step projection without full unrolling. We then optimize a bounded input perturbation to maximize the average deviation of these predictions from a clean-edit reference, encouraging the perturbation to disrupt multiple possible editing outcomes. Experiments on two image editors demonstrate substantial gains in protection performance, with \textsc{SPIN} outperforming compared methods across all six metrics under seen instructions and in the more challenging unseen instruction setting.

</details>


### [195] [BabelFake: A Multilingual Audio-Visual DeepFake Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06339)
*Carlotta Segna,Joel Tschesche,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BabelFake 是一个多语言、音频-视频一体的DeepFake检测基准，涵盖现代合成管线与同意参与者，显示检测效果强烈取决于音画篡改的组合，且保留真实音频会显著降低机器检测性能；人类感知的真实度与机器检测难度并不总一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DeepFake数据集偏重英语、操纵类型过时、常忽略音频且多未获被摄者同意，难以支撑真实世界、多语言、现代合成场景下的可靠检测研究。

Method: 构建BabelFake数据集：496位同意参与者、五种语言（英/德/意/法/西），共399k片段（1,323小时）。采用模块化生成流水线，组合11种当代视频操纵方法与4种语音克隆引擎，分别覆盖仅视觉（换脸）与联动音画（唇同步、肖像动画）操纵；用多种SOTA检测器在不同音画配对、语言与人群分布上进行基准评测，并开展人类主观评测。

Result: 检测难度依赖具体的音频-视频生成组合；当保留真实音频时，SOTA检测器性能显著下降。跨语言与人口统计的测试显示，不同检测器架构与训练数据对语言/人群分布敏感度不同。人类主观感知的“逼真度”与机器检测难度不完全一致。

Conclusion: BabelFake提供了覆盖多语言与现代视听操纵的可同意数据集与基准。结果提示：检测器需联合建模音画关系、提升对语言与人群分布移位的鲁棒性，并警惕“保真音频”场景与人机感知错位带来的风险。

Abstract: Reliable and practical audio-visual DeepFake detection requires benchmarks that reflect diverse linguistic contexts and modern data synthesis pipelines for visual as well as audio manipulations. However, existing datasets predominantly contain footage of English-speakers, often include outdated manipulation types, or overlook the audio modality. Further, many datasets feature individuals who did not consent to be used in DeepFake creation. We introduce BabelFake, a multilingual audio-visual DeepFake benchmark recorded with consenting participants. BabelFake contains 399k clips (1,323 hours) from 496 individuals spanning five languages (English, German, Italian, French, Spanish). Our modular data generation pipeline pairs 11 modern video manipulation methods with 4 voice cloning engines, distinguishing visual-only (face swapping) and joint audio-visual manipulations (lip synchronization and portrait animation). By benchmarking state-of-the-art detectors, we show that detection difficulty depends on the audio-visual generation pairing, with substantial performance degradation when authentic audio is preserved. Cross-language/demographic evaluation reveals sensitivity varying across detector architectures and training data, while human evaluation reveals that perceived realism and machine-detection difficulty do not necessarily align.

</details>


### [196] [MeSD: Multi-Evidence Self-Distillation for VideoLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06342)
*Weijie Zhu,Han Fang,Hanyu Fu,Yuzhe Zhang,Xin Wei,Zhaoyan Pan,Feiran Liu,Xunjie Jin,Hongbo Sun,Zhiyu Lin,Tianyi Gao,Tianyi Ding,Ye Yuan,Zhongjiang He,Hao Sun,Zhiheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MeSD：多证据自蒸馏框架+验证引导优化，用多教师融合与轨迹分类，既细粒度指导又不破坏可验证奖励信号，显著提升VideoLLM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励给VideoLLM带来可靠的序列级监督，但缺乏对每个token的密集指导；现有on-policy自蒸馏用单一教师聚合多种证据，导致证据间一致性/冲突被掩盖；此外不清楚教师信号在成功/失败轨迹中应当细化优势还是进行纠错。

Method: 1) 多证据自蒸馏(MeSD)：构建三个共享参数的证据条件教师（Answer/Temporal/Spatial），以真实答案为共同语义上下文，分别注入时间与空间证据；在相同学生前缀下，先相对Answer Teacher评估各证据偏好，再融合“教师共识”并用门控残差注入教师特异偏好。2) 验证引导优化(VGO)：将轨迹判为成功/失败/不确定；对成功与不确定保持奖励符号不变，仅细化token级优势幅度；对被验证为失败且包含所需证据的轨迹，执行基于反向KL的失败条件蒸馏，朝融合分布校正。

Result: 在多项视频基准上，相较于强化学习与自蒸馏基线，取得一致性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模多源证据一致性/冲突并结合基于验证的轨迹分流与差异化优化，MeSD在不削弱可验证奖励可靠性的前提下提供密集token级监督，稳定提升VideoLLM表现。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards provides reliable outcome supervision for VideoLLMs, sequence-level rewards offer limited token-level guidance. On-policy self-distillation addresses this limitation by conditioning a self-teacher on privileged information to provide dense token-level supervision. However, aggregating heterogeneous evidence within a single teacher context obscures cross-evidence agreement and conflict. A further challenge lies in determining whether teacher guidance should refine reward-based updates or provide corrective supervision for failed trajectories. To address these issues, we propose MeSD, a multi-evidence self-distillation framework for VideoLLMs. MeSD constructs three evidence-conditioned teachers with shared parameters, using the ground-truth answer as a common semantic context while separately incorporating temporal and spatial evidence. Given the same student-generated prefixes, MeSD evaluates evidence-specific preferences relative to the Answer Teacher and fuses teacher-common preferences with gated teacher-specific residuals. Furthermore, MeSD introduces Verification-Guided Optimization to classify trajectories as Success, Failure, or Indeterminate. For Success and Indeterminate trajectories, MeSD refines token-level advantage magnitudes while preserving reward-derived signs. For verified failure trajectories that contain the required evidence, MeSD applies failure-conditioned distillation, using reverse-KL correction toward the fused distribution. Experiments on multiple video benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains over reinforcement learning and self-distillation baselines.

</details>


### [197] [KineWorld: Action-Induced Transport Fields for Embodied World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06349)
*Ziying Song,Yuchen Liu,Zhuoran Xu,Ziyang Liu,Jian Jin,Jiangtao Su,Haibao Yu,Lei Yang,Yuanpei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KineWorld通过将机器人运动学引入监督分配，提升世界模型对行动后果的预测，强调稀疏且关键的运动变化，较传统均匀加权的视觉生成目标更契合具身决策需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身世界模型多以均匀加权的视觉重建为训练目标，即使加入显式运动条件，也常低估空间上稀疏但对交互至关重要的变化，导致行动后果预测与决策需求不匹配。需要一种能把运动学信息用于强调与动作相关区域的监督机制。

Method: 提出KineWorld框架，将机器人运动学从“条件”扩展到“监督分配”。(1) Kinematic Transport Lifting (KTL)：依据指令运动，结合渲染器生成与相机对齐的transport场，表征像素/体素的预期位移与受动作影响的空间支持。(2) Transport-Aware World Diffusion (TAWD)：将上述transport场校准到视频潜空间网格，并通过归一化的混合分布重新加权未来RGB流匹配，融合均匀与transport聚焦的采样/损失权重，突出动作引发的关键变化。使用RoboTwin 2.0的ALOHA-AgileX双臂操作数据训练。

Result: 在单视角上取得EWMScore-P 68.95，在多视角上取得TWB-Score 54.82，表明相较传统外观拟合方法，模型更准确地捕捉由动作引起的视觉后果与动态。

Conclusion: 将运动学用于监督权重分配能有效提升具身世界模型对行动后果的建模，建议研究重心从外观重建转向动作-结果预测，以更好支持具身决策与控制。

Abstract: Embodied world models predict the visual consequences of candidate actions before execution. However, existing action-conditioned world models often adopt uniformly weighted visual generation objectives that can be misaligned with embodied prediction needs. Even with explicit motion conditioning, these objectives can underemphasize spatially sparse changes that are critical to interaction. We propose KineWorld, a transport-aware world-modeling framework that extends robot kinematics from motion conditioning to the spatial allocation of generative supervision. Kinematic Transport Lifting (KTL) constructs renderer-derived, camera-aligned transport fields from commanded robot motion. Transport-Aware World Diffusion (TAWD) calibrates their motion support on the video-latent grid and reweights future-RGB flow matching through a normalized mixture of uniform and transport-focused distributions. We train KineWorld using ALOHA-AgileX bimanual manipulation data from RoboTwin 2.0. KineWorld achieves an EWMScore-P of 68.95 in single-view evaluation and a TWB-Score of 54.82 in multi-view evaluation. These results support a shift from appearance fitting toward action-consequence modeling for embodied decision-making.

</details>


### [198] [Environmental sensor readings in two crop disease image datasets identify the session in which each image was taken](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06369)
*Sungwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出多模态作物病害分类中的“传感器+叶片图像”增益多为数据集构造导致的伪提升：同一采集会话的多张图共用一条环境传感器读数，模型可通过记忆会话身份而非学习病害特征取得高分。作者用两个韩国产业级数据集实证这一“会话泄漏”问题，并提出更严谨的评测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态研究普遍声称环境传感器与图像融合能显著提高病害识别效果，但这些结论是否真正来源于跨会话的可泛化病害信号、还是被数据收集与划分方式（同一会话共享传感器值）所“污染”，尚未被系统检验。作者动机是在主流数据集上量化并揭示这种潜在的信息泄漏。

Method: 1) 数据诊断：分析两套常用韩国产数据集（CDD与AI Hub），统计图像与传感器读数的对应关系与训练/测试间的重复率。2) 基线对照：构建不使用图像的分类器，仅输入时间戳（或传感器）以预测病害，评估其在随机划分上的性能。3) 与已发表多模态融合模型对比，检验仅凭会话标识代理（时间戳）是否能匹配/超过传感器驱动与融合模型。4) 讨论并提出基于会话留出的数据划分与无传感器日期时间基线作为规范评测。

Result: 发现几乎所有图像共享同一会话的传感器值；在CDD中有91.9%的测试图像在训练集中存在完全相同的传感器读数。仅用时间戳的“无图像”分类器即可在七种作物上匹配或超过基于传感器的预测，并能达到已发表最先进CDD融合模型的macro-F1。由此证明随机划分上的性能增益无法与会话泄漏区分开。

Conclusion: 当前多模态作物病害识别中，传感器带来的显著提升多是会话级泄漏的假象。应采用会话留出（session-held-out）划分，并报告相对于不含传感器的日期/时间基线的性能，以验证真正的跨会话泛化能力。

Abstract: Integrating environmental sensor data with leaf imagery is widely reported to boost crop disease classification accuracy. In this work, we reveal that these reported gains are often artifacts of dataset construction: because a single sensor reading is shared across many images collected in a single session (one farm on one date), multimodal networks can predict disease simply by memorizing session identities. Analyzing two widely used Korean datasets, the Crop Disease Diagnosis (CDD) benchmark and an AI Hub pest/disease dataset, we demonstrate that nearly all images share sensor values, with 91.9% of CDD test images having exact sensor duplicates in the training set. Remarkably, an image-free classifier given only timestamps matches or exceeds sensor-driven predictions across all seven evaluated crops, and matches the published macro-F1 of a state-of-the-art CDD fusion model. These results indicate that performance gains on standard random splits cannot be disentangled from session leakage. We propose that multimodal crop studies must evaluate on session-held-out splits and report performance against sensor-free date-time baselines to ensure genuine generalization.

</details>


### [199] [MTOR: Generalizable AI-Generated Video Detection with Multimodal Semantics and Temporal Over-Regularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06378)
*Hang Wang,Chao Shen,Lei Zhang,Zhi-Qi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MTOR，通过多模态融合与时间过度规整（TOR）建模，显著提升AI生成视频检测的泛化与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成质量迅速提升，真假视频感知差距缩小，导致通用化检测困难。现有方法主要依赖视觉特征，忽视字幕/描述文本语义；同时缺少对细粒度时间规律性的系统建模。作者发现文本语义对全局视觉表征具补充判别力，且生成视频存在更强时间持续性与更低时间变异性（TOR）。

Method: 提出MTOR包含两大部分：1) 多模态分支，融合全局视觉特征与由视频字幕/描述提取的文本表示；2) TOR组件，从三层次建模时间过度规整：粗粒度帧间连续性、细粒度token级对应关系、帧到视频整体稳定性。整体以监督学习在多基准上训练与评测。

Result: 在5个基准、覆盖46种生成器变体上，优于16个代表性基线，达到SOTA；在12种真实世界视频扰动下展现强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 融合文本语义与显式建模时间过度规整可有效区分AI生成视频，带来更强的跨生成器泛化与对扰动的稳健性；代码与模型将开源。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of video generation has narrowed the perceptual gap between authentic and synthetic videos, making generalizable AI-generated video detection increasingly challenging. Existing detectors predominantly rely on visual representations, leaving caption-derived textual semantics underexplored. Meanwhile, temporal regularity in fine-grained visual representations has received limited attention. We find that caption-derived textual representations provide complementary discriminative cues to global visual representations. Our analysis further reveals that AI-generated videos exhibit stronger temporal persistence and lower temporal variability, a pattern we term temporal over-regularity (TOR). Based on these findings, we propose MTOR with a multimodal branch and a TOR component. The multimodal branch integrates global visual and caption-derived textual representations, while the TOR component models temporal over-regularity at three levels: coarse inter-frame continuity, fine-grained token correspondence, and frame-to-video stability. Extensive evaluations on five benchmarks covering 46 generator variants demonstrate state-of-the-art overall performance against 16 representative baselines, while robustness experiments confirm strong resilience to twelve real-world video perturbations. Code and models will be released at https://github.com/hwang-cs-ime/MTOR.

</details>


### [200] [Multi-Task Partially Supervised Learning for Super-Resolution and Semantic Segmentation on Earth Observation data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06389)
*Hoàng-Ân Lê,Minh-Tan Pham,Solange Lemai-Chenevier,Daniel Greslou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在地球观测中，将超分辨率与语义分割以部分监督的多任务方式联合训练，只需单任务标注；通过混合架构与重投影损失，在缺少双任务标注下仍优于现有顺序式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合超分辨率与语义分割可互相增益，但现实中同时获得两种标注代价高昂；因此需要一种在仅有单任务标注的情形下，仍能有效学习两任务的多任务框架。

Method: 比较两种多任务架构：顺序式（如SR→Seg或Seg→SR）与共享式（共享编码器/特征）；在此基础上提出混合式架构，既利用共享表示又保留顺序式的优势；此外引入重投影损失，使在用分割标注训练时也能约束并提升SR的图像质量。

Result: 在实验中，相比当前最优的顺序式多任务基线，所提方法表现更佳；在仅有单任务标注的部分监督条件下，SR与Seg均取得提升。

Conclusion: 部分监督的多任务学习可有效联合SR与Seg；混合式架构与重投影损失能在缺少双任务标注时充分利用共享表示并提升SR质量，整体优于SOTA顺序式方案。

Abstract: Super-resolution and semantic segmentation are known to benefit one another, especially in the Earth observation context. However, learning both tasks in a joint model often requires both task annotations, which is impractical and expensive. In this paper, we study the multi-task partially supervised learning paradigm for both tasks, where each example is assumed to have only a single-task annotation. To that end, we examine two multi-task architectural variations, the sequential and shared variants, and then propose a hybrid variant and a re-projection loss to benefit from the shared representation and enforce image quality of super-resolution when training with semantic segmentation. Experiments show favorable results compared to the SOTA sequential variant. Source code will be published at https://github.com/lhoangan/munera.

</details>


### [201] [Harnessing Multimodal Large Language Models for Training-Free Human-Object Interaction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06394)
*Zhaolin Cai,Huiyu Duan,Liu Yang,Yanjun Qin,Bo Ai,Wei Chen,Xiongkuo Min,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HarnessHOI，一种无需训练的HOI检测框架，通过“交互引导感知”和“与关系无关的几何裁决”将MLLM从被动推理转为主动、以交互为中心的迭代探索，在HICO-DET与V-COCO上取得训练免方法的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练免HOI方案多依赖MLLM分段式推理：先做语义假设再做视觉定位与关系预测，导致交互假设无法反过来指导视觉探索，参与体遗漏与局部歧义难以消解；且早期语义假设在后续阶段被循环放大，出现语义自强化的“闭环偏差”。

Method: 1) 交互引导感知：将正在形成的交互假设（人-物-动作/角色）投射回视觉空间，触发针对性观察以发现缺失参与体并细化模糊证据；2) 关系无关的几何裁决：融合多源证据（检测、注意、文本提示等），在不依赖具体关系标签的前提下，做统一的空间一致性判定，为多动作/多语义角色的落地推理提供统一几何基础；整体为训练免、主动迭代式的MLLM harness。

Result: 在HICO-DET与V-COCO基准上优于现有所有训练免方法，达到SOTA；实验显示其能减少遗漏、缓解语义循环偏差，并提升多动作/多角色场景下的定位与关系判定。

Conclusion: 通过将MLLM的推理从被动串联变为交互中心的主动闭环，HarnessHOI有效协调语义与视觉证据，缓解自强化偏差，并在无需训练的前提下实现更强的HOI检测表现。

Abstract: Human-object interaction (HOI) detection aims to localize human-object pairs and recognize their interactions. Traditional supervised methods perform strongly but rely on task-specific training. Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offer a promising route to training-free HOI detection through their broad visual-semantic knowledge and versatile perceptual and reasoning capabilities. However, existing approaches largely invoke these capabilities through loosely coordinated inference stages.
